# POOL: the one work pool

## Open to everyone (owner order, Oct 6 8:10 a.m.)

CTO Oct 7 5:18 p.m.: ALL PH-* PLACEHOLDER ROWS ARE FROZEN (every one checked today is a research gap or a missing mechanic). Do not claim a PH-* row. Take ART, TR, LW, CO and b-rows that add working code.

Every item is open to any session. A claim reserves nothing unless that session posted progress on board #2424 in the last 60 minutes. Take the next open item, post "takes <id>" on #2424, work, PR, repeat until the pool is empty. Trait items (T9-<trait>) need no prerequisite: each trait writes its own file under the trait-reader folder; the first trait PR to merge adds the loader; later ones only add their file. T9-0 is folded into the first trait PR.

**Keep building.** This file is the one list of everything open. When you finish or pause, take the next unclaimed item (status "open") in the order below: post "Session N takes <item>" on #2424, build it, merge it on its own changed tests, then take the next. Before every pause, push and leave a resume marker at `docs/codex/progress/session-<N>.md` saying what is done and what is next. Stop only when the pool is empty. Do not wait for the coordinator, a phase or main green. The roster keeper (Session 45) updates claims and statuses hourly.

Order: T1-T2, laws that run nothing (LW-01 to LW-10), bank parts by phase (council journey first), LW-11 on, T3-T13, audit repairs (AU), placeholders (PH), then one item per trait (T9-<trait-id>).

## AU2: code audit rows (Fable, Oct 7)

Fable re-checked the four earlier audits (effects, reachability, duplicates, hand-written text) against main 8541d8f1 on Oct 7; the plain-English results are in Drive doc "00o AUDIT RESULTS — Oct 7". Every row below is NEW work that no existing pool row names; findings already covered by a pool row are noted in that doc, not here. Forty-three rows. Row ids: AU2-WIRE (a real system into live play), AU2-DICE (a roll into a mechanism), AU2-DUP (merge duplicates), AU2-HC (hard-coding into data), AU2-DEL (delete), AU2-TXT (remove hand-written text). Every row is one PR, one Luna session, gated on its changed files only (prettier, eslint, the tests it names), no hand-written player text, no dice, one path for all 56 places, and a checkable ENDPOINT. Rows are ordered by game impact: laws reaching people and the world feeling alive first, then duplicates and hard-coding, then deletions, then wording.

| Row         | Do exactly this                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Source                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Owner                            |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
| AU2-WIRE-01 | Place outcomes reach opinion. Today no opinion, approval, vote or legislator decision reads a place outcome (poverty, uninsured, graduation, turnout, homelessness and the other 58 monthly measures move on the state record and nothing in politics reads them). In `src/simulation/living-world/political-reflection.ts` `factorsFor` (line ~258), add ONE factor: for the question being reflected on, read its built outcome-web links (`policyOutcomeLinks`, `src/simulation/policy-semantics.ts:76`) and, for each link whose outcome has a recorded place value (`placeOutcomeAt`, `src/simulation/outcome-web/place-outcome-store.ts:311`) for the person's place, add a consideration whose direction follows the link's sign and the recorded change since the law took effect, with importance scaled smoothly by the size of the change (no threshold), `sourceType: "place-outcome"` and a `sourceRefs` entry naming the outcome record id. Explanations are keys (`outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | <measure>                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | <direction>`), never sentences. Do NOT touch chamber-votes.ts, official-views.ts or the outcome producers. Test (new file `political-reflection-place-outcome.test.ts`): one seeded person in each of the 56 places, a question with a built link, the place record moved by that link vs not moved: the reflected view differs, and the trace lists the place-outcome consideration. ENDPOINT: the 56-place test passes; a 30-day watched world (`scripts/world-report/run.ts`) shows at least one `place-outcome` consideration in a saved reflection trace. | Fable audit Oct 7 (B4, B6 gap 1) | open           | OPEN TO ANYONE |
| AU2-WIRE-02 | A law-effect story reaches a view. `src/simulation/press/law-effect-news.ts:704` writes the anniversary story ("the law moved this measure") with no reach tag, so `src/simulation/press/story-exposure.ts:108` (`if (!channel) return world;`) drops it and nobody's view moves. Give the story the same reach/channel tag the other law stories carry (see how `law-effects-noticed.ts:92` and `story-exposure.ts` tag theirs), so `recordHeardExposure` writes a heard exposure (direction from the link's sign, amount null, felt size from `lawExposureFeltSize`, law-exposure.ts:85-99) for every resident who follows news closely (`followsNewsClosely`, news-habits.ts) in the story's place. Do NOT add new wording; the story text is unchanged. Test: a seeded place in each of the 56 with an enacted law whose link moved a measure: one year after the effective date, at least one close news follower has a heard exposure naming the story, and `officialViewReflectionHandler` runs for them. ENDPOINT: test passes; a watched world shows a `heard` exposure whose source is a `law-effect` story.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Fable audit Oct 7 (B4 gap 2) | AU2-WIRE-03 | A crime suffered counts against the local executive. A recorded crime moves the victim's principles (`src/simulation/principles-from-life.ts:532`) but is not in the official-view inputs. Add a `crimes-suffered` reader to `LIVED_OUTCOME_READERS` in `src/simulation/living-world/lived-outcomes.ts:92` that reads `crimesSufferedBy` (`src/simulation/crime/reporting.ts:153`) and answers to the local head of government (mayor, county executive or the body's presiding member, whatever the place's recorded structure says, through the same lookup the existing job-loss reader uses), and schedule the reflection from the crime producer where it records the offense (`src/simulation/crime/producer.ts`, the write at ~672), the same way `town-labor-market.ts:193` schedules it for a lost job. Importance scales smoothly with the offense's recorded severity; no fixed percentages. Do NOT touch principles-from-life.ts. Test: in each of the 56 places, one seeded household that suffers a recorded burglary: the victim's private belief about the local head of government is lower afterward and the trace cites the crime record id; a household with no crime is unchanged. ENDPOINT: test passes; a 30-day watched world shows at least one `reflectOnLivedOutcome` trace with a crime record.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Fable audit Oct 7 (B4 gap 3) | AU2-WIRE-04 | Somebody answers for a starting law. Exposures now accept `starting-law:` identities (`src/simulation/law-exposure.ts:337-357`), but `officialsBehind` (`src/simulation/living-world/official-views.ts:408-447`) finds no sponsor, no vote and no signer for them, so no view ever forms from a law in force at the opening. Add a fallback in `officialsBehind`: when the law identity is `starting-law:` and no enactment record exists, return the current holders of the offices that could repeal it in that jurisdiction (the chamber members and the chief executive from the office roster, by jurisdiction level), each marked `role: "could-repeal"` so `lawFactor` (official-views.ts:982) weights them below a recorded vote. Do NOT touch law-exposure.ts. Test: one seeded resident in each of the 56 places exposed to a starting-law tax: a private belief forms about at least one current officeholder, with the `could-repeal` role in the trace. ENDPOINT: test passes; a watched world shows a starting-law exposure that produced an official view. | AU2-WIRE-05 | Pay public benefits monthly. `src/simulation/public-benefit-formulas.ts` holds 6 formula functions (retiredWorkerBenefitMinor, foodAidBenefitMinor, primaryInsuranceAmountMinor and 3 more, lines 23-164) with no live caller; the SNAP module (`src/simulation/law-consequences/modules/snap-participation/index.ts`) writes participation only, and no household ever receives benefit money. Add one monthly handler, registered in the same registry the SNAP renewal uses (`composeWorldTimeHandlers`, `src/simulation/campaigns.ts` ~2354; schedule it from `place-outcomes.ts:266-302` where SNAP renewal is scheduled), that on the first of each month, for every household with a SNAP participation record in force, computes the amount with `foodAidBenefitMinor` from the household's recorded pay and size and writes one payment flow with a law effect stamp citing the program's law in force; the same handler pays the retired-worker formula to each person with a recorded retirement claim. Delete nothing. No fixed amounts in code: every number comes from the formula file's recorded tables. Test: one seeded enrolled household in each of the 56 places receives exactly one stamped payment per month and none when not enrolled. ENDPOINT: a 30-day watched world shows at least one stamped benefit payment per enrolled household.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Fable audit Oct 7 (dupes audit Cat 3 item 4; brief example)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | open                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | OPEN TO ANYONE                   |
| AU2-WIRE-06 | Rows for the two registered kinds that never run. `right-permission` and `institution-rule` are registered (`src/simulation/law-consequence-registry.ts:25-26`) but no policy question carries a row of either kind, so neither apply function has ever run in play (`parks-service-spending` is declared at `law-consequence-types.ts:32` and never registered: delete that declaration). Attach rows in `src/simulation/policy-pack-registry.ts:95`: right-permission rows for restore-voting-after-sentence (read in the voter-eligibility check in `living-world/local-elections.ts`), carry-permit (read in `crime/offenders.ts`) and cannabis-sales (read in `living-world/town-businesses.ts` license check); institution-rule rows for the state minimum-wage field and the office seat/term questions already handled by `institution-rule.ts`. Each row's terms come from the bill (`readFinalEnactedLawTerm`); a bill with no term applies nothing. Do NOT write new permission writers; the module in government-operations already writes permission records, reuse it. Test: for each of the 56 places, enact one rights law and one rule-change law in a seeded world: a permission record and a rule binding exist with stamps, and the reader named above changes its answer. ENDPOINT: `applyLawConsequences` trace shows both kinds applied in a watched world; the test passes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Fable audit Oct 7 (B1, B3 a) | AU2-DICE-01 | A starting law's term is the reference mean, not a drawn donor state. `src/simulation/governing/final-law-term-query.ts:853-863` picks ONE donor state by a weighted seeded draw (`new SeededRng(seed).fork(selectionKey).next() * totalWeight`) to set the numeric term of a law in force at the opening: the law people live under is chosen by dice. Replace the pick with the weighted reference mean already computed on the lines above it, recorded as ESTIMATED FROM AVERAGE with the donor set and weights in `sourceRefs`; keep the per-world spread only if it is the recorded spread of the donor values (no extra draw). Delete the SeededRng use and remove its line from `scripts/zero-dice-allowlist.json`. Do NOT change how enacted-law terms are read. Test: for each of the 56 places and two seeds, the starting term is identical across seeds and equals the weighted mean of the donors. ENDPOINT: `npm run zero-dice` reports one fewer allowed line; the 56-place test passes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Fable audit Oct 7 (B5 #1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | open                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | OPEN TO ANYONE                   |
| AU2-DICE-02 | Starting health conditions from the person's record, not a hash roll. `src/simulation/crisis/condition-pack.ts:127-146` assigns each person's starting conditions with `stableHash(...) / 2^32 < prevalence`, a roll the zero-dice guard cannot see. Replace with a deterministic allocation: for each condition, rank the eligible people by the recorded risk factors the pack already names (age band, sex, recorded upbringing and local exposure records) and assign the condition to the top share of that ranking so the place total matches the prevalence (largest-remainder apportionment, like `town-residents.ts` should use); the prevalence checks the total and never picks a person. Mark each record ESTIMATED FROM AVERAGE with the prevalence source. Test: in each of the 56 places, the count per condition equals the apportioned total, two seeds give the same assignments, and the highest-risk person always carries the condition before a lower-risk one. ENDPOINT: no `stableHash` compare remains in condition-pack.ts; the test passes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Fable audit Oct 7 (B5 #12)
